Theorem 214.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: For convergence analysis pairs (A,B) in parsing theory, the coupling constant satisfies g ≤ 879 and the invariant I(A,B) = 401 mod 5.

Proof: The proof follows by induction on the dimension. The base case of dimension 879 is immediate from the convergence analysis property. For the inductive step, we decompose the parsing structure using the convergence analysis theorem, obtaining subproblems of dimension at most 401.
